@@8427284 <h> April 23rd , 2016 02:00 PM <p> United Nations Secretary-General Ban Ki-moon . AP FILE PHOTO <p> India on Friday signed the historic Paris climate agreement here along with more than 170 nations , marking a significant step that has brought together developing and developed nations for beginning work on cutting down greenhouse gas emissions to combat global warming . <p> Environment Minister Prakash Javadekar signed the agreement in the UN General Assembly hall at a high-level ceremony hosted by UN Secretary-General Ban Ki-moon . The ceremony was attended by heads of government , ministers , corporate leaders and artists . <p> " This is a moment in history . Today you are signing a new covenant with the future , " Ban said , adding that , " We are in a race against time . " <p> ADVERTISEMENT <p> The opening ceremony included music from students of New York 's Julliard School and a short video bringing the " gavel moment " from Paris to the signature ceremony . <p> At 171 nations , the signing ceremony for the climate agreement set the record for @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ day , previously set in 1982 , when 119 countries signed the Law of the Sea Convention . <p> The signing is the first step toward ensuring that the agreement comes into force as soon as possible . <p> After the signing , countries must take the further national ( or domestic ) step of accepting or ratifying the agreement . <p> The agreement can enter into force 30 days after at least 55 Parties to the UNFCCC , accounting for at least 55 per cent of global emissions , ratify the agreement . <p> India has maintained that the burden of fighting climate change can not be put on the shoulders of the poor after decades of industrial development by the rich nations . <p> It has announced plans to quadruple its renewable power capacity to 175 gigawatts by 2022 as part of the government 's plan to supply electricity to every household . <p> Finance Minister Arun Jaitley , in an address to a city- based think tank earlier this week , had said that notwithstanding its development need , India is completely committed to protecting the climate . @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ far , still the hard reality is we have a lot of distance to cover . We need more housing , power , toilets , roads and factories . <p> Therefore our requirement of fuel is certainly going to increase . Notwithstanding that our own standards of protecting the environment are very rigid , " Jaitley had said . <p> " There is a method in each one of the steps we are taking " like taxing oil , cess on coal and emphasis on alternative renewable energy , he had said . <p> " We are conscious of our responsibilities , " he said . <p> " Most people who looked at the global situation say that if we do n't succeed in maintaining the world under a 2 degrees Celsius rise , then it 's going to be incredibly difficult to realize the Sustainable Development Goals , " Nabarro warned . <p> " And so implementing the Paris agreement is important for promoting prosperity , improving people 's wellbeing and protecting the environment . The Paris Agreement was adopted by all 196 Parties to the UN Framework Convention on @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ on December 12 last year . <p> A day before the signing ceremony , Javadekar had said that India has come to New York for signing the Paris agreement with " full confidence that ultimately the collective wisdom will prevail and all countries -- developed and developing -- will do their bit . " <p> He said that every climate action has a " cost " and developed nations are asking the developing world to pay that cost . <p> " The developed world has caused the climate change of today because of their 100 years of relentless carbon emissions . We are much cleaner at our development stages as compared to those of the developed world , " he said , adding that the developed world " behaved irresponsibly " and the developing nations are suffering . <p> India , which along with the United States and China , is world 's top greenhouse gas emitter , has maintained that its energy needs will be enormous going forward as it needs to lift millions out of poverty , develop its infrastructure and provide basic amenities like toilets , affordable houses @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ a legal formality , with only Heads of State or Government , foreign ministers , or other representatives with " formal powers " from their governments signing the agreement . <p> In the agreement , all countries agreed to work to limit global temperature rise to well below 2 degrees Celsius , and to strive for 1.5 degrees Celsius . <p> The signing ceremony coincided with the International Mother Earth Day and in his message on the day , Ban said the Paris accord , in conjunction with the Agenda for Sustainable Development , holds the power to transform the world . <p> There is only one original copy of the agreement -- it contains the full text of the agreement in the six official languages of the United Nations -- Arabic , Chinese , English , French , Russian and Spanish . <p> There is a page for each of the 197 Parties to the UN Framework Convention on Climate Change . <p> Each nation signed the agreement while seated at a table on a specially constructed stage in the UNGA hall . <p> French President Francois Hollande began the @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ week , David Nabarro , the Secretary-General 's Special Adviser on the 2030 Agenda for Sustainable Development and Climate Change , told reporters that the signing of the Paris Agreement is crucial because achieving progress in relation to climate change is central to the broader effort of achieving the SDGs.

@@8430833 <p> Welcome to Rappler , a social news network where stories inspire community engagement and digitally fuelled actions for social change . The US actor and UN Messenger of Peace speaks at the High-Level Event for the Signature of the Paris Agreement at the United Nations Headquarters in New York . Photo by Andrew Gombert/EPA <p> UNITED NATIONS , USA -- Hollywood actor and environmental campaigner Leonardo DiCaprio urged world leaders signing the Paris climate deal on Friday , April 22 to deliver on their commitments to cut greenhouse gases , telling them : " The world is now watching . " <p> " You will either be lauded by future generations or vilified by them , " said DiCaprio at the UN ceremony where 171 nations lined up to sign the landmark accord . <p> " We can congratulate each other today , but it will mean absolutely nothing @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ beyond the promises of this historic agreement . @@8432897 <h> Miriam : Renewable Energy Act is not serving its purpose <p> As the country gears up for the third and final presidential debate in Dagupan city this afternoon , Sen. That should not be the case . What we are doing is ensuring that whatever gains we get now will be enjoyed by generations to come , " Santiago said . <p> Leaders from 175 countries signed yesterday the Paris Climate Agreement at the United Nations headquarters in New York City . <p> Santiago 's statement comes amid accolades from at least three coalitions of environment groups for championing resource conservation , climate change adaptation , and disaster risk reduction in her program of government . <p> GREENEST CANDIDATE <p> She was named the " greenest candidate " by the Kalikasan People 's Network for the Environment for her track record @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ Roxas II the " meanest " to the environment . <p> Santiago was also lauded by a green group initiative called Luntiang Bayan : Boto Para sa Tao , Hayop at Kalikasan as the presidential candidate who has done the most for the environment , citing the numerous laws and bills she drafted . <p> The lady senator also topped the scorecards launched by the Green Thumb Coalition on Friday , bagging 79 percent in " people-centered sustainable development , " 78 percent in " human rights and integrity of creation , " and 50 percent in " climate justice . " <p> In her platform of government , Santiago highlighted the need for evidence-based planning . <p> " To combat climate change , we must practice both mitigation and adaptation , and our decisions must be based on data and science , " she added . <p> Santiago also vowed to capacitate local government unit ( LGUs ) to better implement environment protection , climate change and disaster risk reduction programs ; take advantage of indigenous knowledge ; and foster transparency in the sector . <p> REVISIT EPIRA <p> The @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ Reform Act ( EPIRA ) , which she said allowed the market to dictate the country 's energy mix , and the Mining Act , citing research showing the cost of mining activities outweighs the benefits . <p> " As president , I will fully and wholeheartedly implement the Renewable Energy Act , which I authored and sponsored in the Senate . The law is not working as it should be . It has been in operation for six years , but action has been slow , and our renewable energy supply remains small , " Santiago said . <p> She also vowed to push for the passage of the National Land Use Act , which can help in the management and efficient use of energy resources , and the Energy Efficiency Act , which encourages firms to improve energy use practices . <p> " We must look at environmental issues as development issues to prevent a detached approach in policy-making . We must realize that environmental degradation and climate change impact our health , economy , and survival , " she stressed .

@@8434284 <h> DOLE awards over P205 million to 7,917 workers through SENA settlement <p> April 24 , 2016 <p> MANILA , April 24- Labor and Employment Secretary Rosalinda Dimapilis-Baldoz said that speedy settlement of labor disputes in the first two months of 2016 through Single Entry Approach ( SEnA ) , an alternative dispute resolution mechanism of the Department , which resulted in the grant of P205,766,580.88 in settlement benefits to 7,917 workers . <p> " The fast settlement of the request for assistance at the DOLE Regional Offices and attached agencies shows their commitment to the conciliation and mediation mode of dispute settlement . Their efforts led to the timely and efficient dispute settlement using the DOLE 's flagship program , the Single Entry Approach mechanism and other alternative modes of dispute resolution , " said Baldoz . <p> SEnA is an administrative approach that provides a speedy , impartial , inexpensive , and accessible settlement procedure of all labor issues or conflicts to prevent them from ripening into full-blown disputes . Initiated by Baldoz in 2010 through Department Order No. 107-10 , the SEnA became effective that @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ a law , R. A. 10396 . <p> Baldoz , citing a report from Executive Director Atty . Shirley Pascual of the National Conciliation and Mediation Board ( NCMB ) , said that of the total monetary benefits facilitated by the DOLE Regional office and attached agencies amounting to P205 , 766,580.88 , the amount of P150 , 635,616 was facilitated by the Department 's Regional Offices . It benefitted 5,054 workers , or a per capita benefit of P27,638.38 . <p> On the other hand , the attached agencies facilitated a total of P55,130,964.37 monetary package for 2 , 413 workers , or a per capita benefit of P22,847.47 <p> Executive Director Pascual also said a total of 13,397 request for assistance ( RFA ) were filed at the DOLE Regional Offices and attached agencies which are implementing the SEnA program during the said period . Of the said RFA , 3,626 were filed at the DOLE Regional Offices and 10,311 RFA were filed at the attached agencies , the bulk of which , or about 6,131 were filed at the National Labor Relations Commission ( NLRC ) . <p> @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ NCMB , Overseas Workers Welfare Administration , Philippine Overseas Employment Administration , and DOLE-Legal Service . <p> Majority of the new RFA were filed in labor intensive regions , particularly at the National Capital Region which recorded 1,157 RFA , or 39 percent of the total RFA filed at the DOLE Regional Offices . This was followed by Region 4-A , with 429 RFA , or 14 percent ; Region 11 , with 327 , or 11 percent ; and Region 3 , with 177 , or six percent . <p> The total number of RFA filed at the Philippine Overseas Employment Administration ( POEA ) and Overseas Workers Welfare Administration ( OWWA ) marked an increase of 45 and 56 percent compared to the 650 and 831 RFA recorded during the same period in 2015 , respectively . <p> According to Pascual 's report , the combined disposition rate of the DOLE Regional Offices and Attached Agencies during the same period was 66 percent , a decrease of two percent from last year 's 68 percent . The settlement rate was 56 percent which was the same rate covering the @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ Offices and attached agencies also disposed the filed RFA in less than 30 days , or ranging from one to 25 days duration , except in DOLE Regional Office No. 2 with 69 days . <p> DOLE Regional Office No. 3 settled the RFA fastest , or with an average of one day only . Fifteen of the 16 DOLE Regional Offices settled the RFA within five to 25 days , or an average of 12 days . Attached agencies , on the other hand , settled the filed RFA within seven to 25 days . <p> A total of 7,436 RFA , or about 53 percent are still on process of conciliation-mediation proceedings under the SENA program . @@8436957 <p> The Philippines will ratify the landmark Paris Agreement , a legally-binding accord to combat climate change , during a signing ceremony in New York on April 22 . <p> Environment Secretary Ramon Paje will represent President Aquino and deliver the Philippine statement at the UN headquarters on April 22 , which coincides with the celebration of Earth Day . <p> Negotiated by 195 nations in France last December , the Paris Agreement calls for keeping the average global temperature to well below 2 degrees Celsius while pursuing efforts to limit temperature increase to 1.5 degrees C above preindustrial levels . <p> 2017 deadline <p> Climate ambassadors have until April 17 , 2017 to sign the deal , and the agreement will come into force once signed by at least 55 countries . <p> The Philippines has pledged to reduce carbon emissions by 70 percent in 2030 through greater use of cleaner and renewable energy , improved mass transport services , more efficient waste management , reforestation and forest protection , and co-firing biomass , Paje said . <p> Senator Loren Legarda hailed the formal signing of the @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ catastrophic effects of climate change by limiting temperature increase . <p> Climate justice <p> Legarda , the UN Office for Disaster Risk Reduction Global Champion for Resilience , made this statement when she addressed the Climate Vulnerable Forum in New York late Wednesday . <p> Legarda called for " climate justice for the victims of extreme weather events , " apparently referencing the thousands of drought-hit farmers who protested in Kidapawan City over the lack of government subsidy and support . The four-day protest ended in a violent dispersal on April 1 that claimed the lives of three farmers . <p> " Is it not ironic and sheer paradoxical that the very people who till the land so that others may have their share of rice on their tables are now begging for food ? " Legarda asked . Inquirer.net

@@8454930 <h> Natural disasters since 1900 ' have cost $7 trillion ' <p> By : Agence France-Presse April 25 , 2016 7:56 AM <p> More than a third -- 38.5 percent -- of the economic damage , and just over half the loss of life was the result of flooding , according to James Daniell , an Australian risk engineer at the Karlsruhe Institute of Technology in Germany . This photo is of flooding in Pakistan , 3 April 2016 . REUTERS <p> The death toll from such natural calamities -- which also include earthquakes , volcanoes , droughts , heat waves , and wildfires -- topped eight million from 1900 to 2015 , according to findings presented at a meeting of the European Geosciences Union in Vienna . <p> More than a third -- 38.5 percent -- of the economic damage , and just over half the loss of life was the result of flooding , according to James Daniell , an Australian risk engineer at the Karlsruhe Institute of Technology in Germany . <p> " Flooding is the key driver " for economic loss and death , @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ years , the largest such database in existence . <p> Since about 1960 , storms and storm surges -- the exceptional waves they cause -- have replaced flooding as the most destructive forces , battering buildings and infrastructure . <p> Whether this shift was due to climate change was impossible to say , he told AFP . <p> " When we go back in time , the record is not complete , " he said on the sidelines of the annual gathering of about 13,000 Earth and space scientists . <p> " We probably have floods and storms from the 1930s or 1940s , for example , that never came into the database . " <p> The frequency of other natural disasters -- notably earthquakes , which accounted for 26 percent of losses , and volcanoes , which caused one percent -- remained fairly constant over time . <p> Earthquakes accounted for nearly 30 percent of deaths , some 2.3 million people over the 115-year period . <p> Of those fatalities , nearly 60 percent died when buildings collapsed , while about 28 percent perished in a tsunami or landslide . @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ to natural disaster has increased progressively over time , the study found . <p> But as a percentage of the rising value of all infrastructure on the ground -- currently estimated at some $300 trillion -- losses have actually declined , Daniell said . 
@@8456846 <h> Sanchez : Solve one , create a problem <h> Sanchez : Solve one , create a problem <p> SOLVE a problem , create another one . Negros Occidental currently hosts seven solar power facilities , making it the Philippines ' solar power capital . The province has a total solar generation capacity of 341.5 megawatts , according to the province 's Investment Promotions Center-Public Information Section . <p> Yet being the solar capital has its challenges . The first is that of land use . The Carnegie Institution of Washington suggested combating global climate change by increasing the use of renewable energy resources such as solar . Yet solar power plants could compromise protected natural habitats . <p> Such facilities use vast swaths of land for solar gathering and generating equipment . Until now , studies quantifying the effects on land-cover change and analyses of impacts on protected areas near solar facilities have been limited . <p> Bacole ? a Dr. Anabel V. Villanueva , an organic restaurateur , agrees . While she " will take solar power versus coal anytime because of the impact to the @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ perfectly arable land , which can improve food security , into solar panel subdivisions for at least 25 years at a time . " <p> Solar plants use lands directly occupied by solar arrays , access roads , substations , service buildings , and other infrastructure . Bacolod 's proposed 200-megawatt solar power plant will use a 140-hectare lot in Barangays Felisa and Vista Alegre . <p> In fact , nearly 20 percent of solar facilities were far 10 kilometers from transmission infrastructure . That means energy must travel farther and therefore have greater energy losses . They cost more to build . And new transmission corridors degrade the natural environment . <p> To which Edwin Marthine " Ted " Lopez of Alter Trade Foundation answers in the same Facebook thread , " you can always build higher and well-spaced platforms for the panels and plant beneath it with rootcrops or low leguminous crops ( such as peanuts ) . <p> No problem with that . The problem is the power distribution will be through the electric cooperatives who still charge high rates to the consumers . Now who can appreciate @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ question of who benefits ? Anabel asks , " this production of so-and-so kilowatt hour goes to Imperial Manila or Cebu and we still suffer outrageous electric rates , poor service , and blackouts . Who wins ? " <p> Blame the energy sources for blackouts . Last Friday , Metro Bacolod experienced a 13-hour unscheduled power interruption in some of Ceneco 's area of coverage when its 69-kilovolt wire and pole in Barangay Vista Alegre fell down . Affected were Ceneco 's substation feeders in Bacolod , Talisay , and Silay cities . <p> Still , these issues are birth pains . Solar power as a renewable source is a lot better than coal-fired plants . Which is a better option : huge land spaces that can produce clean energy ? Or coal-fired plants that use small land spaces but emits greenhouse gases ? In the era of climate change , the choice is obvious. @@8460078 <p> MANILA - The International Maritime Organization 's Marine Environment Protection Committee ( IMO-MEPC ) on Monday endorsed the application of the Philippines to designate the world-renowned Tubbataha Reefs Natural Park and World Heritage Site as a Particular Sensitive Sea Area ( PSSA ) . <p> Permanent Representative to the IMO Gilberto Asuque submitted the application at the plenary meeting of the 69th session of the MEPC held at the IMO Headquarters in London on April 20 . <p> The decision of the Philippines to designate the Tubbataha as a PSSA under IMO Guidelines is aimed to protect the biodiversity and ecological sensitivity of the area from the threats of international shipping . <p> Tubbataha is located in the Sulu Sea , about 80 nautical miles from the island of Palawan , and is a living laboratory for the study of marine ecological processes and climate change adaptation . It lies at the heart of the Coral Triangle , the richest biogeographic region in the world . <p> Tubbataha encompasses an area comprised of a 97,030-hectare " Core Zone " and a 350,000-hectare " Buffer Zone " surrounding @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ a UNESCO World Heritage Site , and was also listed in 1999 in the Ramsar List of Wetlands of International Importance . <p> Since 2009 Tubbataha has been designated by the Philippine government as a national marine protected area through Republic Act 10067 . <p> The area is significant in terms of its unique ecological , socio-economic , scientific , and cultural characteristics . Its extensive coral reefs and associated ecosystems are very vulnerable to damage from international shipping activity , in particular physical damage and pollution arising from operational discharges and maritime incidents , as well as the introduction of alien invasive species . <p> The application will now be endorsed to the Maritime Safety Committee ( MSC ) for the consideration and approval of protective measures to be implemented in the area of the TRNP . These IMO-sanctioned measures are designed to further protect the area from the hazards of international shipping . The Philippines is proposing an " Area To Be Avoided " by ships and other watercraft over 150 gross tons plying the area . <p> MSC and MEPC are expected to finally approve the designation and @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ assisted in the presentation of the proposal by Commodore Elson Hermogino , Commander , Marine Environmental Protection Committee ; Capt . Rolando Punzalan , Technical Adviser ; and Emma Sarne , Alternate Permanent Representative to IMO . Prof. Jay Batungbacal of the UP College of Law assisted in the technical preparation of the proposal .

Foreign hackers allegedly perpetrated the laundering of $81 million from the Central Bank of Bangladesh through several Philippine-based casinos and Chinese nationals . The US Federal Bureau of Investigation ( FBI ) is probing into the biggest cyber heist at Bangladesh 's formal request . <p> Clearly , some national problems can not be solved by a country all by itself . However , fulfilling this commitment will be meaningless if the other 195 countries who agreed to the Paris accord will not follow their so-called Intended Nationally Determined Contributions . <p> The sluggishness and slowdown of today 's global economy have started in China and in the developed West . But that does not mean Asean will be immune from its knock-on impact . <p> The essential point I 'm suggesting is that international problems surely require cross-border cooperation for solutions . But even seemingly domestic matters , like tax evasion , drug trafficking @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ assistance and collaboration of friends abroad . <p> In one word , the nation must foster good relations and mutually beneficial arrangements with other countries to face down some of our biggest internal challenges . And as the official representative of and spokesman to the international community , the next president should be at the forefront of cultivating these good relations .

" " It 's really to reduce bureaucracy , provide consistent laws and tweak taxation to be more competitive with Asean ( Association of Southeast Asian Nations ) neighbors , " Barcelon said . " That covers a lot of areas like airports , roads , ports , telecommunications and irrigation , " the PCCI chief said . <p> A is about boosting agriculture , the sector that employs about a third of the population . <p> N refers to a " new era " of manufacturing , as PCCI envisions a country that will be able to build more factories and attract more foreign direct investments . @@8470122 <p> Shell has unveiled a concept city car which , if it were ever to go into production , could deliver material reductions in energy use in the road transport sector . Here 's the news from Shell : The three seater car is tangible proof of energy efficiency improvements that can be achieved by using cutting edge technology available today through a process of " co-engineering " whereby vehicle body , engine design and lubricants are all created together . <p> Independent testing and a rigorous life-cycle study shows that Shell 's Concept Car would deliver a 34% reduction in primary energy use over its entire lifecycle when compared to a typical city car available in the UK . The Shell Concept Car would use around half the energy required to build and run than a typical small family car available in the UK and 69% less than that of a typical sports utility vehicle available in the UK <p> The Shell Concept Car is a total rethink of the Gordon Murray Design T.25 city car produced in 2010 for which Shell produced a prototype oil to @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ is the result of a co- engineering collaboration between world leading vehicle , engine and lubricant designers , with each of the three elements of the vehicle tailored to work optimally with each other . It takes a holistic view on energy reduction focusing on design material selection ; reduced energy demand via aggressive downsizing , and streamlining while enhancing the efficiency of energy delivery through innovative engine design and lubricant formulation to minimise the impact in terms of overall energy lifecycle use . <p> The car 's gasoline consumption has been measured using a range of vehicle testing protocols covering both steady state and urban driving styles . Sample test results include a steady state consumption of 2.64Litres per 100km 38km/Litres at 70kph and an improvement of 4.67g CO2/km on the New European Driving Cycle ( NEDC ) from the use of bespoke lubricants , equivalent to a 5% improvement in fuel efficiency compared to standard lubricants available in the UK . <p> Mark Gainsborough , Executive Vice-President of Shell 's global lubricants businesses which backed the project said , " This is a significant automobile engineering milestone . I @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ partners at Geo Technology and Gordon Murray Design have achieved . Insights gained from this project could be transformational in terms of how we address energy use in the road transport sector . Energy use and climate change are major issues for society . This project shows that if we use the best of today 's technology , including cutting edge lubricants science , we could potentially have a major impact on energy use and reduce CO2 emissions . The improvement in economy derived from the collaborative design of engine and lubricant is impressive and highlights the enormous benefits achieved from close relationships between design partners . It also shows the powerful role that lubricants can potentially play in helping achieve CO2 reduction targets . " <p> The Shell Concept Car was independently tested at a UK certified automotive testing facility alongside a range of other cars under comparable conditions to measure fuel economy and CO2 emissions . In the formal NEDC test the Shell Concept Car produced lower CO2 emissions than both a typical petrol-powered city car ( 28% ) and a hybrid car ( 32% ) . <p> Shell @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ designing ' the motor oil to complement and enhance the overall efficiency of the vehicle , principally by minimising friction . Shell 's Lubricants technology team created bespoke engine oil , based on its premium product Shell Helix Ultra with PurePlus Technology . In parallel , engine guru Osamu Goto 's group at Geo Technology optimised the three cylinder petrol engine by re-designing and optimising many of the internal engine components associated with friction . On the cold portion of the NEDC , these fluids reduced CO2 emissions by 7.1% and on the combined cycle by 5.0% , compared to standard lubricants available in the UK , again highlighting the value of co-designing engine and fluids . <p> Built around Gordon Murray Design 's patented iStream platform , the Shell Concept Car represents a radical rethink on the way in which cars are designed , developed and produced . It combines cutting-edge lightweight technology -- the car weighs just 550kg -- and is built using carefully chosen materials which have a low energy and CO2 footprint . Gordon Murray 's experiences in Formula One ? racing have been used to develop @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ number of the car 's components were created using 3D printing to accelerate the construction of this prototype vehicle . The car also uses recycled carbon fibre for its body that can be assembled for a quarter of the price of a conventional steel car and almost the entire car can be recycled at the end of its life . The car makes use of a modified version of Shell 's Drive App via a smartphone . This App provides the driver with real time feedback via an on-screen graphic which emphasizes the fact that fuel consumption is highly dependent on driver 's behaviour . <p> From a styling perspective , the Shell Concept Car offers a new take on the ' tall and narrow ' look , and dials up the fun factor with its sporty central driving position and two passenger seats behind . The design produces an extremely novel seating arrangement allowing three people to be carried despite the car 's diminutive exterior dimensions and gives it a turning circle smaller than that of a London taxi , making it ideal for urban driving . <p> Dr. Andrew @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ team said : " Our car may be small , but it 's packed with potential . @@8501788 <h> Betrayal by the automakers <h> Betrayal by the automakers <p> MAFIA boss John Gotti once said " I never lie because I fear no one . You only lie when you 're afraid . " <p> Volkswagen was clearly afraid that genuine emissions testing would show their diesel engines were no cleaner than the next . So they lied and rigged the tests . But seven months ago they were caught red-handed . <p> Last week , VW announced it is proposing to buy back or repair 600,000 vehicles in the US affected by the scandal . Owners will have the option to have their cars fixed or bought back by VW . Early indications are that most owners want shot of their VWs . <p> Their deception is costing VW dearly -- on Friday the company took a $18.28-billion charge related to the scandal forcing it to slash its 2015 dividends and post a deep loss . <p> Shortly after the scandal broke , Emissions Analytics Ltd , a vehicle emissions data service , revealed that on-the-road testing showed that diesel cars from Mercedes-Benz , Honda @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ road versus the lab . <p> And the latest miscreant in this long streak of automaker dishonesty is Mitsubishi with the news last week that it 's also been cooking the emissions books . <p> On Wednesday , Mitsubishi announced it had falsified fuel economy test data to make emissions levels look more favorable . Almost immediately its shares slumped more than 15 percent , wiping $1.2 billion from its market value . <p> The test manipulation involved 625,000 vehicles produced since mid-2013 . <p> These include its eK mini-wagon and eK Space as well as 468,000 similar cars it made for Nissan . Mitsubishi says no cars sold in the Philippines were involved . <p> In a statement issued to the Sun.Star , Mitsubishi said the company has apologized to its customers and has immediately halted production and sales of the four cars involved . But this may only be the start of Mitsubishi 's problems . US regulators are now involved . <p> The crisis is spreading . Germany 's top carmakers say they will recall 630,000 vehicles to fix diesel engine software technology that has been blamed for @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ British government announced it had road-tested the 37 top-selling cars in Britain and none of them passed the emissions tests . But none broke the law because the legal test only applies in a laboratory . <p> Technicians discovered that emission levels were startlingly higher in the real world -- i.e. on the road -- compared with the lab . <p> Diesel engines produce much higher levels of air pollutants than gas-driven engines , although they produce less carbon dioxide . This has led European countries to encourage drivers into diesel cars , reducing the impact of driving on climate change , but vastly increasing the problem of air pollution . <p> It 's ironic that the push for lower carbon dioxide emissions to combat climate change has led to higher air pollution . New research is revealing the extent of the damage being done routinely to our health . <p> About seven percent of deaths are caused or contributed to by air pollution , according to the World Health Organization , and the effects on people 's quality of life is even greater . <p> Long a silent killer , @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ , particularly on small children and older people . <p> Published in the Sun.Star Bacolod newspaper on April 27 , 2016 . <p> Latest issues of Sun.Star Bacolod also available on your mobile phones , laptops , and tablets . ang designated Paje as the Philippines ' signatory to the agreement . <p> The Paris agreement seeks limiting global greenhouse gas emissions ' rise to well below two degrees C so the world can avoid dangerous climate change . <p> Among the measures Paje cited as necessary for helping guard against threats to PAs are integrating conservation of these areas in @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ financing and management . <p> " As government workers , responsible individuals and Filipinos , we need to keep up with demands of the present and push forward day by day , " he noted . <p> He also said decision-making for PA protection and management must be science-based . <p> DENR spearheaded the conference to provide a venue for experts and authorities concerned to look into how to better sustain ecosystem services and benefits from the country 's PAs . <p> Among the conference 's expected outputs are documentation of PAs ' contributions in sustaining ecosystem services and benefits as well as compilation of good PA management practices , noted BMB Director Theresa Mundita Lim . <p> " One other expected output is networking of partners on PA management , " she said .

De Guzman gave a rundown of the city 's eco-friendly highlights , including a smoothly run materials recovery facility and waste management program ; an " Eco-Savers " passport program for students who bring in recyclables and get groceries in return ; a " todosipsip " program , or free septic tank desludging ; a clean-up waterways program ; and a dedicated emergency warning system , among many others . <p> Mayor De Guzman also proudly mentioned the bike-friendliness of his city , which has 82 kilometers worth of bike lanes as of December 2015 . Cities , being economic drivers , are needed for a @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ more drivers outside Cebu and Davao , " he said . <p> As the private sector co-chair of the NCCP , Luz outlined some ideas and points of actions to help build these resilient cities , including a sustainable cities academy , online training , and a " livable cities " design challenge or an urban planning competition . <p> His group is also looking at a Philippine Disaster Recovery Foundation operations center , " an office with stand alone facilities , a coordination center operated twenty-four-seven ; with easy access to ports , airports , with provisions for stockpiling and parking . " <p> Art , science , and development <p> The policy work was balanced out by DaanRoosegaarde , a Dutch artist and innovator who blends good design with purpose , and engineering with imagination . One of his simpler designs , for example , is a bike path made of glow-in-the-dark material that gets charged by the sun during the day . When it glows , the patterns are reminiscent of Van Gogh 's Starry Night . <p> Roosegaarde and his team of experts are aware of " @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ depends on your definition of art , " Roosegaardepointed out after his presentation . " It is the source of who we are , a way of thinking . " Citing the bike paths , " they 're energy friendly , attractive to people , attractive to business . So it 's not about art , it 's about finding new connections . But art can be a great incentive . " <p> What places like Metro Manila would most benefit from today are inventions like his " Smog-Free Tower , " which he dubbed " the biggest air cleaner in the world . " <p> Inspired by the energy generated by static electricity , the seven-meter contraption runs on patented ion technology and sucks up 30 cubic meters of smog every day . It runs on green wind energy and uses no more electricity than a water boiler ( 1,400 watts ) . The air measured around it is 75% cleaner than before . <p> To fund it , Roosegaarde and his studio made " Smog-Free Jewelry " -- rings and cufflinks with compressed smog particles encased in a diamond-like @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ in execution . <p> He concluded with a thought on collaboration -- that designers , scientists , non-profits , and business leaders would do well to look at how public spaces can be improved . " A public space is one of the most important places to start , because it 's the place everybody uses and cares about . " <p> He posed the challenge to develop " spaces that are more clean , where people canfeel the difference . The city we live in becomes much how we feel like , what our visions are . @@8510039 <h> Disaster response center launched in Visayas <p> April 28 , 2016 <p> QUEZON CITY , Apr. 28 -- The Visayas Disaster Response Center ( DRC ) was inaugurated in Mandaue City , Cebu to enhance emergency response capacity in the Philippines . The center houses -- among other things -- a custom-built mechanized system designed to rapidly produce 50,000 family food packs in a day , which is enough to feed 250,000 people for three days . <p> The center is a continuation of the strategic partnership between the Department of Social Welfare and Development ( DSWD ) and the United Nations World Food Programme ( WFP ) . It is funded by the governments of Australia and the United Kingdom . <p> Inside the 5,000-square meter center is a custom-built mechanized system designed to rapidly produce 50,000 family food packs in a day , which is enough to feed 250,000 people for three days . Combined with the mechanized production system at the National Resource Operations Center in Pasay City , this will double family food pack production capacity to feed 500,000 people for three days @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ for improved storage of food packs and warehousing of food and non-food commodities , guaranteeing quality stockpiles of family food packs and other humanitarian assistance ready for distribution . Spaces for training sessions and offices are also available . <p> The DRC was conceived as a result of the lessons learned from the response to Typhoon Yolanda . The said typhoon badly hit the Visayas region and challenged the government in responding to large-scale humanitarian relief requirements . <p> " Typhoon Haiyan showed that disasters can strike anywhere in the Philippines , " said Praveen Agrawal , WFP Philippines ' Representative and Country Director . " The inauguration of the Visayas disaster response center is a significant step towards decentralizing the Philippine government 's emergency response . With help from our donors , WFP will continue to support the Philippines by establishing respective DRCs in Luzon and Mindanao . WFP is also providing technical training to key government representatives on disaster response logistics and supply chain management . <p> The Philippines is considered as one of the most disaster-prone countries in the world exacerbated by climate change , with various natural disasters affecting the country such as typhoons , droughts , earthquakes , and volcanic activities . " <p> El Ni ? o is triggered by periodic oceanic warming in the eastern Pacific Ocean that can trigger drought in some regions , heavy rain in others . <p> Much of Asia has been punished by a bone-dry heat wave marked by record-high temperatures , threatening the livelihoods of countless millions . <p> Vietnam , one of the world 's top rice exporters , has been particularly hard-hit by its worst drought in a century . <p> In the economically vital Mekong Delta bread basket , the mighty river 's vastly reduced flow has left up to 50 percent of arable land affected by salt-water intrusion that harms crops and can damage farmland , said Le Anh Tuan , a professor of climate change at Can Tho University . <p> More than 500,000 people are short of drinking water , while hotels , schools and hospitals are struggling to maintain clean-water supplies . <p> Neighboring Thailand and @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ water and Thai rice output curbed . <p> In Malaysia , the extreme weather has shrunk reservoirs , dried up agricultural lands , forced water rationing in some areas , and caused repeated school closures as a health precaution . <p> Fisherman Abdul Rafar Matarrh said his daily catch in central Malaysia 's Pahang River has been decimated as the normally broad river has shrunk to a third its size , leaving dead fish to rot in the dry bed . <h> More from this Category : <p> COMMENT DISCLAIMER : Reader comments posted on this Web site are not in any way endorsed by The Standard . @@8551044 <h> Velez : The thing called ' pagbabago ' <h> Velez : The thing called ' pagbabago ' <p> PAGBABAGO . Pagbag-o . Change . Every election we hear that word from every candidate 's lips who promised heaven and from every person longing for a better life and a genuine government . Thirty years since EDSA , we still search for that thing . <p> This 2016 , after six years of another Aquino in government and all its shortcomings , are we going to see the change coming ? <p> The biggest draw for change has been Rodrigo Duterte , who has been the public 's choice as the alternative to the Aquino administration 's failures and incompetency to fight corruption and poverty , fanned by his call to end corruption and crime in three to six months . <p> The conduct of his campaign has been a game-changer , as pointed out by fellow columnist Andy Uyboco who said his campaign is run more by a machinery comprising of netizens and ordinary folks beating out the traditional machinery of money , image-and spin-makers and traditional @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ a possible Duterte presidency , it is mind-boggling why they are picking a Marcos in the vice presidential race . The analysis from Rappler showed that Bongbong Marcos is favored among middle aged voters who saw the Marcos ' era as a time of ' peace ' and ' progress ' . <p> The thing is , people still choose leaders through sentimentalism , as what they did in 2010 with Noynoy riding on a wave of sympathy over the death of his mother Corazon Aquino . Bongbong is riding on that ' solid North ' and sentiments of old folks for a ' quieter ' era . But that also points to Noynoy 's failures in Manila to solve crime , poverty and traffic , as well as problems such as disasters and peace in Mindanao . It 's ironic that the tables are turned between Marcos and Aquino , which shows that people will ride on politicians who can challenge the status quo with words and machinery . <p> There 's no change in the senatorial race where old-timers and come-backing senators are topping the surveys . You 'll @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ opposed the Reproductive Health Bill on religious grounds ; Franklin Drilon the incumbent LP stalwart mired in the overpricing of the Iloilo convention center ; and Manny Pacquiao who has drawn the ire of LGBT and supporters over his anti-LGBT remark . <p> The need for new faces and alternative agenda in the Senate through the likes of Neri Colmenares is still wanting with this kind of setup . This survey thing is a disadvantage by turning the senatorial race to a popularity and name-recall contest . <p> Here in Davao City , the local elections is really quiet . Congressional representatives in two of the three districts are running unchallenged , Karlo Nograles in District 1 and Mylene Garcia-Albano in District 2 . <p> So what 's the score with change in this scenario ? It 's an uneven inconsistent view of the change we want . People may like Duterte for the promise of change . But he has to contend with a machinery and system that favors the elite that holds influence to the local levels . Then there 's the threat of ' digital high-tech cheating ' @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ 's database . <p> Perhaps the equation of change is not simply just voting for the right candidates . We have to be part of that equation of breaking down the elite machinery . That comes in small steps from the local arena by empowering communities and sectors up to the national stage where we pushed for policy change . Many issues such as education , health , agriculture , climate change , peace for lumads and Moro communities , need to be pushed not just by politicians but by the people themselves . <p> Latest issues of Sun.Star Davao also available on your mobile phones , laptops , and tablets . @@8564820 <p> Welcome to Rappler , a social news network where stories inspire community engagement and digitally fuelled actions for social change . Rappler comes from the root words " rap " ( to discuss ) + " ripple " ( to make waves ) . <h> 10 reasons why Grace Poe is my president <p> I write this with 10 days to go before Election Day . These are the 10 reasons why Grace Poe is my president and why you should choose her too . <p> 1 . Grace Poe has a clear vision and solid platform of government that builds and improves on " Daang Matuwid ( Straight Path ) . " <p> Senator Poe has a vision for the country : one where no one is left behind . To achieve that , government must be compassionate . " Sa gobyernong may puso , walang maiiwan ( No one will be left behind under a compassionate government ) " captures the two pillars of the senator 's vision . A Poe presidency will focus on poverty alleviation coupled with inclusive , sustainable , and resilient @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ by a culture of excellence achieved through open , accountable , and competent governance , free of corruption , which operates under the rule of law and upholds human rights . <p> On her first day , she has promised to promulgate an executive order institutionalizing freedom of information in the executive branch , among others . She will also order the preparation of a comprehensive feeding program for public schools as one of her first acts . Agriculture , public health and easing the transportation crisis will be priorities in the first 6 months . <p> A Poe government will build on the good governance gains of President Aquino 's Daang Matuwid administration but it will depart radically from the latter in being solutions-oriented , inclusive , and compassionate . A Poe administration will also be different from the Aquino government in its emphasis on human rights and social justice . She will be more committed to labor rights and not just ending contractual labor . Her support for OFWs is also stronger . We will see a lot more empathy in a Poe administration , especially for children and @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ the environmental , climate change , energy and transportation programs of Senator Poe . She has the most progressive positions on issues like the implementation of the Paris Agreement , capping coal power , promoting renewable energy , judicious management of the country 's minerals , agriculture , and fisheries , and marine and aquatic resources . She understands that traffic is not about cars but about mobility and is willing to implement and scale up innovative programs for pedestrians , cyclists , and commuters . <p> I am also sure that a President Poe will appoint the most qualified justices to the Supreme Court . Because of her direct experience of the legal system , Poe knows the importance of competent , independent and ethical justices and judges . I am so excited about her plans to completely support the Chief Justice 's judicial reform programs . <p> It 's not a perfect platform . It has some gaps , for sure . But it is the best that is being offered . <p> 2 . Grace Poe is organized disciplined , diligent , and hard working . <p> In @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ on a range of issues , I have personally seen Grace Poe 's work ethic . The Senator is tireless . She is always prepared for our meetings . She takes time to read voluminous briefing papers prepared for her . She reaches out to experts whenever necessary . <p> Poe 's personal discipline is exemplary . She usually comes to meetings on time and when she is delayed , she respects us enough to let us know . The discipline extends to her staff . She manages them strictly while always treating them respectfully . Clearly , Senator Poe is a performance and outcomes oriented leader . <p> As President , she will set time-bound strategic goals and will hold herself and her team accountable to achieving them . Being a good manager and an excellent organization person , she will not tolerate division in her team and will make sure everyone act as one . There will be no factionalism in a Poe administration because she will not allow it . <p> 3 . Grace Poe is a woman , mother , wife , and daughter . <p> It @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ down on women as the weaker sex , to be joked about , and to be ignored . Double standards that marginalize women and favor men prevail in all areas of our society -- in politics , in economics , in our culture . It would be so good to have a woman lead the effort to change that . <p> In a Poe presidency , many women will be appointed to the cabinet and to all government positions and the judiciary . My hope is that more than half of the cabinet will be women and that by the time her term is over , more than half of the Supreme Court will be women . That would be so great . <p> That Grace Poe is a wife , mother and a daughter is one of the things that attracts me very much . She is fully engaged with her family , attending recollections and graduations even at the height of the campaign . In fact , regardless of where she is in a given day , Poe almost always flies home to be with her children and husband @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ mother . A Poe presidency will be good for families because she understands what we are all about . <p> 4 . Grace Poe is a foundling and will always prioritize children and the human rights of all . <p> Her life story is one of the most compelling reasons why people are drawn to her . My friend , children 's rights advocate Eric Mallonga describes why her presidency would be so good for children : <p> This is a selfless , generous , kind , caring woman , who equally cares about children other than her own . Having a president who would introduce free lunches , even other meals , at schools and day care centers for children is the best thing that could happen to this society . Parents would be able to work and not worry about bringing home enough money for the food of their children because those children would not be hungry . The Grace Poe administration would have already guaranteed their daily nourishment . Filipino children would be well nourished and thus , properly educated because they would not be starving as they @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ who would prioritize that every child would have a suitable family environment , whether their own biological family or an alternative foster , guardian or adoptive family to properly nurture their children , will be one excellent president for this nation . Having a president who would care for the children and provide proper schools for them , regardless of whether they live in the hinterlands or metropolis , whether they are Muslims , Igorots , Lumads , or Christians , regardless of their ethnicity or religion , is one president who can unify our people because of the commonality of causes in raising their families and seeking the best for their children . <p> Having a president who aspires to build not just schools but proper habitats and shelters for every family so that they could properly attend to their children 's needs is the best thing in bridging the great continental divide between the oppressive wealthy and the disenfranchised people and finally resolving the polarization that continues to threaten the stability of our nation . <p> I look forward to a president whose number one priority is children because @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ as a child . Because she has a direct experience also of the importance of human rights , a President Poe will be a strong advocate of the human rights of all , including the mothers of disappeared political activists or OFWs who are imprisoned or sentenced to death in foreign lands . <p> 5 . Grace Poe ran an ethical and effective campaign ; she will also be an effective and ethical president . <p> When the campaign started , I wrote an article for the Ateneo de Manila alumni magazine Fabilioh and proposed that in addition to the candidates ' record of service , it is important to look at how they are handling themselves in the campaign . I observed : " Presidential campaigns are grueling and challenging , beset by crisis . The behavior of the candidates -- their fortitude , determination , discipline , ability to communicate , etc -- is a good way of assessing their effectiveness as leaders . " <p> I would add that presidential campaigns also reveal the moral fiber of the candidates . On this , I am sure , Grace @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ to do personal attacks and go dirty . There have been moments , of course , when she has flared up and lost her temper , when the attacks against her and her family become abusive , but these have been far and few in between . Most of the time , she has been calm and serene and has had the same effect on those who work with her . <p> I have never seen Senator Poe panic even in the face of crises and what seem to be overwhelming challenges . I saw only a grounded and recollected Poe when it seemed she would lose her disqualification cases . That 's what I see in her now , a time when she is trying to catch up in the polls . <p> 6 . Grace Poe will be a listening president . She brings a fresh perspective and new ideas to the presidency . <p> In all my dealings with Senator Poe , I have seen how she is a good listener . She has internalized core values of good governance -- personal integrity , inclusiveness in designing policies @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ the need for social accountability , and a preference for the poor and marginalized . But outside these values , Poe is open to new ideas and inclusive social innovation . A big pleasure in helping draft her platform and preparing her for debates is that nothing is off the table . Every idea can be considered and weighed . I like that in a president . <p> I am definitely not worried that Grace Poe has the least number of years in government experience among the candidates . Because she knows how to listen and because she is an adherent to complete staff work and the highest standards of excellence , the decisions she will make will be well considered . <p> Poe will be a listening President , not only on policy and governance matters , but also on an interpersonal basis . She also values complete staff work and reaching out to a range of views before making decisions . Moreover , all major decisions will be characterized by transparency and inclusiveness . Given that , I am confident that there are enough safeguards to ensure that governance decisions will be made on the merits and not because of access . <p> Her stint in @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ @ with the Estradas and Binays did not stop her from supporting the PDAF investigations . Grace Poe will be a servant leader president who will inspire all of us to serve our county . <p> Watching her speeches in her campaign sorties is always a joy . One , of course , must have an open mind and generous heart to appreciate that and not rely on biases and prejudgments . Her rhetoric is good , soaring even , and she means it . I choose a country where we are united and where no one feels inferior and disadvantaged .
